# Re-verification of the `unit:` rows whose spec's case names changed in the merge of v0.1.46

Compared at `e123f0da5` (HEAD when the work started; the brief names `f8d8ccb57`, which differs from it only in notes) against `3662ca0ea`. Rows: 97, taken from the table of `docs/gpui-port/notes/RETRIAGE.md` by the note "spec: case names changed". Cases are counted with the generator's `CASE` expression; a case's title is its first string literal (after the table for `it.each`); a case is new when its (title, occurrence) is not at the base, retitled when it is new and its body matches a removed case's body (token Jaccard ≥ 0.5), body-changed when its whitespace-normalised text up to the next case differs (this over-reports: a block inserted after a case marks it changed; every such case was read against `git diff -U3`).

## Totals

- Rows kept: 0. Rows lowered: 96 (in `lowerings.json`). Already partial and short of cases: `unit:src/features/vim/__tests__/conflicts.test.ts` (not in `lowerings.json`; a proof to write is in its section).
- Cases at HEAD: 1368. Covered: 978 (657 by an unchanged case's same-name native test, the rest read). Missing: 390.
- Rows marked `[m]` (model only) in RETRIAGE.md among them: `unit:src/features/graphql/graphql.test.ts`, `unit:src/editor/lsp/links.test.ts`, `unit:src/editor/lsp/semantic/kotlin/index.test.ts`, `unit:src/features/color-preview/colors.test.ts`, `unit:src/features/plugins/indexedMarkers.test.ts`, `unit:src/features/settings/__tests__/window.test.ts`, `unit:src/features/templates/__tests__/pluginPackages.test.ts`.
- Every citation in `lowerings.json` is a full test name present in `rust_tests.txt` (checked by `build.mjs`; problems found: none).

## Notes

The reading was done by eight forks of the reader, one per group of feature folders (`work/g1.json` … `work/g8.json`, verdicts in `rows/`). The reader re-read and lowered seven cases the forks had counted covered: `debugger/__tests__/attach.test.ts` cases 0, 1 and 4 (native asserts less than the spec), `executionState.test.ts` case 13 (the variables epoch), `ExecutePopup.test.tsx` cases 3 and 7 (the native tests assert the old Enter behaviour that `ExecutePopup.tsx` dropped upstream), and `updates.test.ts` "reports a failure with Retry, and a cancellation" (its new not-recorded assertion has no native test). Other verdicts are as the forks reported them.

Every lowered row has at least one new or retitled case missing; 30 missing cases are unchanged since the base and were previously argued away in a proof (DocSync 12, imeInput 12, remote 2, build 2, gradleRun 1, i18n 1). The new proofs drop those arguments; the manager may restore them.

Native tests that assert the behaviour the merged webview no longer has (wrong, not just missing): `run_testing::services::tests::defaults_to_spring_boot_and_persists_the_users_list_per_project`, `run_testing::services::tests::hides_a_configuration_unless_it_runs`, `run_testing::services::tests::lists_added_types_fully_and_other_types_only_with_their_runs`, `run_testing::services::tests::does_not_apply_a_picker_result_after_switching_projects`, `run::chooser_view::tests::lists_pinned_configurations_first_in_pin_order_and_not_again_in_their_group`, `run::chooser_view::tests::proofs8_chooser::lists_the_configurations_started_last_after_the_pinned_ones_newest_first`, `vcs_ops::ignore::tests::opens_nothing_for_the_local_exclude_file`, `vcs_ops::git_log::tests::disables_history_rewriting_for_pushed_commits`, `workbench::log_actions::tests::the_commit_menu_disables_history_rewriting_for_a_pushed_commit`, `vcs::log_filters::tests::groups_head_local_and_remote_branches_and_leaves_out_the_remote_head`, `agents::task_actions::tests::keeps_placement_out_of_session_menus`, `agents::composer::tests::picks_the_main_settings_and_cycles_the_mode`, `platform_more::remote_settings::tests::normalizes_unsupported_types_to_sftp`, `plugin_models::decorations::tests::filters_project_roots_and_unknown_styles_and_replaces_tree_badges`, `plugin_models::indexed_markers::tests::hides_markers_on_edit_and_when_the_entry_goes_away`, `build_tools::execute::tests::starts_page_up_on_a_completion_rather_than_a_section`, `build_tools::execute::tests::page_down_selects_a_completion_rather_than_a_section_at_its_landing_position`; and the settings pages test that expects "Accent colour" where the spec now expects "Accent color" (see the `pages.test.ts` section).

### `unit:src/features/run/services.test.ts`

Status now: done ([x]). Cases: 44 at base → 46 at HEAD (RETRIAGE.md: 44 → 46). Covered 33 of 46.

New: groups configurations by their folders first, as Run/Debug Configurations does; keeps the type out of Services; does not duplicate docker-compose, docker-dockerfile or docker-image runs in the run configurations provider; counts one per listed running configuration; leaves out types not added and Docker kinds, and counts a live debugger; does not count a configuration removed from the window while it runs.

Removed: orders remembered configurations when their type was not explicitly added; persists the ran configuration ids per project; lists previously run configurations without a session; ignores ran ids whose configuration is gone and merges sessions.

Retitled: lists added types fully and other types only with their runs → lists added types fully and keeps other types out; shows Spring Boot by default, hidden while the project has none → shows nothing for a type that was never added; defaults to Spring Boot and persists the user's list per project → starts empty and persists the user's list per project; hides a configuration unless it runs → hides a configuration even while it runs; keeps an empty default group whose configurations are all hidden → keeps an added type's group whose configurations are all hidden.
